**Q:** when does Most-Favored-Nation go in to effect

**A:**

→ **July 24, 2026** at 12:01 a.m. ET, taking effect alongside the new Section 301 forced-labor tariffs that replaced the expired Section 122 surcharge.

→ **Net-of-MFN calculations** apply to economies like the EU, Taiwan, Japan, South Korea, and Switzerland, capping the combined duty structure at 10% or 12.5%.

→ **Postal shipments** face normal MFN duties for the first time starting the exact same day.

**Q:** can pharmaceutical survive operating in markets outside of the United States of America

**A:**

→ **Yes, but under severe margin compression:** Ex-US markets across Europe and OECD nations rely on direct price controls, universal healthcare mandates, and external reference pricing that cap drug profitability far below U.S. levels.

→ **The U.S. profit dependency:** While the U.S. makes up roughly **50%** of global pharmaceutical sales revenue, it generates the vast majority of net income and acts as the essential funding engine required to amortize multi-billion-dollar R&D pipelines.

→ **Strategic defensive adjustments:** Operating outside the U.S. demands strict cost containment, local manufacturing, and staggered launch sequencing to prevent international price caps from bleeding into global revenue corridors.

Sources:
